# Skills · E · part 84

792 entries, excalidraw-deck to excel-lookup-strategies. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **excalidraw-deck**: Make a hand-drawn Excalidraw slide deck with animations (slides sketch themselves in, steps reveal items stroke by stroke, a dot travels along arrows, spotlight/dim, captions) from a small JS spec. Outputs a self-contained HTML deck, an editable .excalidraw (one frame per slide), an Obsidian .excalidraw.md and a PNG per slide. Use when asked for a presentation, pitch deck, slides or animated diagram "in Excalidraw" / "hand-drawn". ([kolyakr/hacktoberfest](https://github.com/kolyakr/hacktoberfest/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/excalidraw-deck) · ★1)
